Class Commands

HomeWikiMap prepCharacter builderNPC builderTown builderTreasure plannerEncounter builderChange LogClass CommandsGM Screen

What each class and archetype can do with the bot: its slash commands, and the abilities its Combat tab and {action} offer.

Each game system has its classes and then its archetypes. Abilities are listed for a character who has the class at 20th level and every one of its feats; yours depends on the level, feats and choices you have. Open a heading to see its commands. An archetype is listed once it has a command or an ability.

Pathfinder 2e286
